GCW — Pay-per-view

NC-17 VI, 4 February 2009

4 February 2009
Content noticeA period document, served unedited. It carries the humor, language, and content typical of its era and community — some of it crude, dated, or offensive, including material that wouldn't be written today. See Curation.
NC-17 VI University of Phoenix Stadium — Phoenix, Arizona ---- Introduction ---- A Slight Change In Scheduled Programming The lights in the arena dimmed down at the sound of a violin playing. And I heard as it were the noise of thunder One of the four beasts saying come and see and I saw And behold a white horse And it's name that sat on him was Death And hell followed with him The familiar introduction sends the crowd into jeers, almost so much that they don't hear the unfamiliar song. New entrance theme "Clubbed To Death" by Rob Dougan plays over the arena as the crowd greets the Global Championship Wrestling Commissioner with rabid hatred. Cool, confidant, calculating... Christian Zenith looks as if he didn't even realize they were there as struts to a stop on the stage. He looks across the capacity crowd at University of Phoenix Stadium through a pair of black Oakley sunglasses, that hide his emotionless cadet blue gaze. Wearing a custom made black suit with black tie, Christian starts a strut down the aisle towards the ring... fans angrily shouting towards him as he just marches past. Reaching the bottom of the aisle he makes a sharp left turn shrugging off one fan's attempt to reach out and touch the Fortune 500 mainstay. A couple steps and a small walk across the apron was in order before The Conscious Crisis could step through the ropes and into the squared circle, not too much unlike the roped octagon he dominated in GLOBAL Wrestling. Bryan: We were told that we'd be starting the night here with an official announcement, but we were under the impression it'd be from President Steven Caldera... not necessarily Christian Zenith. Yale: I'm a little surprised that Mr. Zenith is the messenger here as well. I'm more curious on what it's about. Bryan: Maybe an attendance record? Yale: Maybe he's stripping Jordan White of the GCW World Heavyweight Champion. Bryan: Maybe you need to quit dreaming over there buddy. Standing in the middle of the ring he cricles around, looking out across the crowd before bringing the microphone up to his lips stopping the new entrance theme. Zenith: I'd like to take this opportunity to welcome the entire world to the biggest show on the face of the planet... GCW's NC-17. The crowd gives a mixed reaction, unaware of Zenith's intentions as he continues on. Zenith: I'm here to start off this Pay-Per-View with a formal announcement regarding tonight's card, and the disciplinary actions that are going to be taken on Chris Bagwell as a result of his attack on Markus Blaire at WorldWide 104. Although still mixed, the cheers are growing stronger as he pauses to open up a written statement from his suit jacket pocket. Zenith: Due to Chris Bagwell's unprovoked attack on non-GCW employee Markus Blaire backstage at WorldWide 104 here in Phoenix... he will be punished by taking away his right to compete here tonight at the biggest show in the history of this company. Therefore the "I Quit" match between Chris Bagwell and Vivica J. Valentine will not be happening here tonight. We at GCW are sorry for any inconvience this may have caused, but as I'm sure you all understand when the rules and warnings of staff members are not followed... consequences for those actions must be enforced. Thank You. The crowd erupts into boos as Christian Zenith folds the sheet of paper back and begins putting it into his pocket as the crowd begins to chant... Crowd: FUCK YOU ZE-NITH! CLAP! CLAP! CLAPCLAPCLAP! Crowd: FUCK YOU ZE-NITH! CLAP! CLAP! CLAPCLAPCLAP! Bryan: Oh boy... When I quest my flashing sword and my hand takes hold of judgement I will take vengance upon mine enemies and I will have paid those who haze me Oh Lord raise me to thy right hand and count me among thy saints... The crowd almost blows off the retractable roof off the top of the stadium as Christian Zenith stops in his tracks to the sound of "Image Of The Invisible" by Thrice. Wearing a #11 baby-doll type home Arizona Cardinals jersey and a pair of black and red Tripp pants, the former GCW World Heavyweight Champion Vivica J. Valentine doesn't waste any time jumping through the curtain to stop what was going on inside of the ring. Valentine: Whoa, whoa, whoa, whoa, WHOA! Vivica exclaimes cutting off her own music as Christian Zenith was already almost out of the ring. Valentine: Are you KIDDING me?! He goes and attacks Ayake Sonoda... and you do absolutely nothing. He attacks whatever those two ring crew member's names were... and you do nothing. He invites my ex-boyfriend backstage, and beats him senseless and you punish BOTH OF US. Are you FUCKING kidding me?! Please tell me this is a joke. Christian stares back at The Fearless Phenom who is obviously pissed off. It wasn't the first time he'd been in this position with her, and it probably wouldn't be the last. Zenith: You see when you run a business, part of your job is to keep your employees in-line. One bad employee can give your company a bad name for years to come. If I want to keep Global Championship Wrestling on top of this industry this is the type of tough decisions that need to be made. I'm sure you understand... Miss Valentine. His voice was very cold and condescending, so much so that the crowd started the usual "ASSHOLE" chant that seemed to follow him wherever he went. Valentine: No, I don't think you understand... Chris. Tonight here in Phoenix, Arizona... I'm kicking Chris Bagwell's ass to Tempe and back whether it's "scheduled" or not. He's put a mark on every single person who I have any sort've relationship with in my life, and he's not just going to walk away from it without my right boot smeared with his blood. I'll do whatever it takes to teach his ass a lesson about fucking with people's personal lives. If that means I'm throwing his ass into a light pole in the parking lot, so be it. The crowd roars with approval as she goes on. Valentine: Because I think there are a couple hundred thousand people in the arena here tonight who showed up to watch Chris Bagwell get his ass kicked so bad, that the 162 strippers and 2 prison "pals" across the world he's given herpes to will feel it. And I'm going to give it to them, whether you "book" it or not. Comprende? Bryan: HELLO. Yale: That was a little unnecessary... The crowd cheers on as Vivica J. Valentine adds one last thing. Valentine: And I'll fucking do it. And these people will go out and watch. I PROMISE you. Again the crowd erupts in approval of what she's saying. It's at this point in time Christian Zenith sees Vivica's promises may be somewhat accurate. Not wanting to lose any ratings and the chance that a mistake like that may cost him his job, he goes on the defense. Zenith: You know what, I don't think we see things the same way. I think it's because you're exceptionally more confidant in your abilities to beat Chris Bagwell than I am in your ability to get the job done. But none the less, if you're going to threaten to take your ball and go play somewhere else... I suppose I'll allow it take place inside the arena tonight, since you're going to do it anyway. But as far as being a part of NC-17, Global Championship Wrestling not sanctioning this match. The crowd cheers as Christian Zenith backs down and a smile crosses the face of Vivica J. Valentine. Valentine: Now that's more along the lines of what I wanted to hear... and don't you worry Chris. He'll be punished tonight. I guarentee it. "Image Of The Invisible" by Thrice kicks back in and Vivica J. Valentine backs out of the arena, wearing a smirk on her face as Christian Zenith simply shakes his head in the ring. Bryan: The "I Quit" Match between Vivica J. Valentine and Chris Bagwell tonight is now "Unsanctioned" from the point of view of Global Championship Wrestling. What a turn of events that is! Yale: Aside from Vivica J. Valentine and Chis Bagwell not getting paid, since officially they're not competing on the NC-17 card... what effects will this have on the match? Bryan: Whatever rules that may have been in place as far as GCW is concerned with the "I Quit" match are also gone. Which means this match won't end with a referee stoppage, Dave. Someone is going to have to say the words, "I Quit" for this thing to end. Any referee that participates in this match will be of his own free will, he won't be assigned to the match. Yale: So he doesn't have the authority to stop it from GCW. Wow, that's going to be huge. Bryan: It certainly makes things a lot more interesting. One of those two wrestlers will say "I Quit" tonight. They don't have a choice now. Yale: I know it JB, I can't wait for Vivica to say "I Quit!" Bryan: She certainly appears motivated here tonight, Dave. That's not going to be an easy task. ---- Vassago & Cleopatra vs. Samson & Delylah Yale: I'm not sure if anyone is going to have an easy task here tonight, JB. Bryan: Well Ladies and Gentlemen, it’s time for the first match of the night! These two teams have the distinction of opening up what could prove to be the biggest show in Global Championship Wrestling history! Let’s hope they’re up to the task. Yale: Well JB it’s a massive weight for anyone’s shoulders, let alone Samson & Delylah and the Den of Iniquity. These four competitors will be responsible for setting the tone for the rest of the evening; we want to get off to the best possible start tonight, and what better way to do that than with a blockbuster opener? Bryan: Well they certainly aren’t lacking in the pedigree department, Dave! Samson and Delylah are former GCW Tag Team Champions, and the Den of Iniquity of Cleopatra and Vassago are no slouches either! They bring a decent amount of history to the table here in GCW, and while we may not have seen the best of them just yet, you know they’ll be chomping at the bit for a chance to impress at GCW’s flagship event! Yale: It is true that we haven’t exactly seen a lot of these four athletes recently, but this is quite possibly the biggest night of each of their professional careers, and hell, in front of this audience, you could coax a potentially show-stealing performance from even someone as low as Scott Stevens himself! These two teams should have no trouble... DING! DING! DING! Focus turns to the centre of the ring where a suited and booted Joey Andrews stands, beaming from ear-to-ear, microphone in hand. Andrews: Ladies and Gentlemen, the following tag team match is scheduled for one fall, and it is the opening match of the evening! The fans whoop and cheer, brimming with anticipation and eager to get the biggest show of the year underway. The houselights dim as purple and gold hues illuminate the entrance ramp as middle-eastern strings seep through the speakers. A harder, more abrasive guitar riff kicks in and pyros explode as Paul Samson and his partner Delylah appear at the top of the ramp, slowly approaching the ring. Bryan: Well here come the former Tag Team Champions, perhaps with something to prove tonight! They haven’t had a great deal of luck since dropping the straps to Oblivion and after a highly-credible title run we haven’t exactly heard much from either Paul Samson or Delylah... Yale: They’ve been quiet, JB. There’s no doubt about it, but these two have always looked like a threat when they’re together and there’s no doubt that they’ll give it their all tonight. There’s a certain degree of ill will between these two teams, and the popular Samson and Delylah will be out to prove their doubters wrong tonight! The duo stop in the middle of the ramp as Paul flexes his impressive physique and Delylah squats down in front of him. Slowly Delylah rises, threading her arms through Paul’s, before the two of them break the pose and begin slapping hands with the fans. Andrews: Introducing first, weighing in at a combined weight of 454lbs! Hailing from Pico Mundo, California and Cairo, Egypt respectively, they are former GCW Tag Team Champions... SAMSOOONNN AND DELYYYYYYYYLLLLLLAAAAAAHHHH! Paul Samson parts the middle and top rope, allowing Delylah to squeeze through as Ra’s "Do You Call My Name" begins to fade away. The downset, eerie tones of Marilyn Manson’s "Sweet Dreams" cover draw a smattering of jeers from the crowd as the lights dim again and Cleopatra and the bald-headed "Demon" Vassago step out onto the top of the ramp. Bryan: Well the last time we saw these two they were interfering in Samson and Delylah’s handicap match against Tessa Windsor a few weeks back, costing S&D the pinfall. Not too much is known about Vassago and Cleopatra but they do seem hell-bent on making Samson and Delylah’s life here in GCW a living hell. What do you reckon of them, Dave? Yale: I know that they’re a couple of diabolical, twisted individuals, JB, and that’s about it. We’ve seen them wrestling for other North American promotions in the past, but this is actually the first time we’ll ever have seen them officially stepping foot inside a GCW ring. Let’s see how they cope. The Den of Iniquity reach the bottom of the ramp, Vassago hissing and sneering at his seething opponents. The referee beckons Samson and Delylah back as Vassago and Cleopatra stand just a couple of feet away from the ring, taunting them. Bryan: Wow! Samson and Delylah look really up for this one, Dave! We could have quite a fight on our hands... Yale: Indeed... Samson looks like he wants to squash Vassago’s shiny head like a grapefruit! Andrews: ... aaaaaand their opponents! From Sin City, Nevada and Cairo, Egypt respectively, weighing in at a combined weight of 483lbs, the team of Vassago and Cleopatra... THE DEEEEENNNNNN OF INIQUIIIIITTTTTTTTYYYYYYYYYY! Finally Samson and Delylah recede, knowing, perhaps, that Vassago and Cleopatra aren’t going to get in the ring with them barking like a couple of caged animals. Finally the Den of Iniquity enter the squared circle – Vassago clambering over the top rope and Cleo slipping under the bottom – and pace around their chosen side, not taking their eyes off their opponents. Bryan: Well this should be interesting; two teams each combined a perfect blend of pace and power! We’ve got the big, powerful bruiser and the petite high flyer on both sides making for what should be an incredibly balanced match-up. Yale: There’s no doubt about it. So Paul Samson might be a little bit bigger than Vassago, but other than that there aren’t that many differences between these two when it comes to the vitals. Let’s get this one started! The bell sounds, and the ladies take to the apron. Samson and Vassago start the match in the ring, circling a few times as they size one another up. Bryan: Looks like it'll be the big men kicking things off here, and-- Just as the two are preparing to lock up, the lights drop and the entire stadium plummets into darkness. The massive crowd roars as cell phones and lighters flash to life in the vast, empty space. Bryan: Oh no... We've lost power... Yale: Are we still on? Bryan: Fans, we apologize, this isn't exactly the best way to start the biggest event of the year... Yale: I dunno, JB, we've seen this before... Bryan: What are you-- Suddenly a hideous, grinding electronic sound rips through the air. It's followed by the sound of television static, drowning out all other noises. Finally the ring is illuminated in a pale blue light, revealing Paul Samson lying face down in the ring! Vassago looks up to find a solid black figure in the ring before him. Wearing a wide-brimmed hat and trenchcoat, face masked in black, the figure appears to stare at Vassago for a long moment as the crowd grows concerned. Bryan: Oh my God, you're right Dave! It's the man in black! That mysterious man in black is back! Yale: We haven't seen him for weeks! He cost Jordan White his match against Garbage Bag Johnny back at WorldWide 100, but since then, nothing! Bryan: And now he's taken down Paul Samson! He's got Vassago in his sights! Vassago charges, throwing a wild clothesline. But the man in black nimbly sidesteps him, grabbing his free arm and pulling him back in. The man in black twists Samson upside down and promptly drops him on his head, eliciting a groan of pain from the stands. Bryan: Oh God, inverted piledriver! He just laid out Vassago! Yale: He's absolutely destroying our opening match, JB! The man in black stands and turns, and Cleopatra flies off the top rope with a cross body. The man in black throws a vicious forearm that connects with her face, cutting her out of the air. Delylah attempts to sneak in behind him, but he catches her with a swift back kick to the gut, then lifts her over his shoulder and delivers a devastating standing spinebuster. Bryan: My God, this man in black has just annihilated Samson and Delylah AND the Den of Iniquity! They're all down! This match never even got started! With the crowd buzzing and booing wildly, the man in black adjusts his hat and remains motionless in the ring. Suddenly a bright flash of light explodes from the turnbuckles, followed by the hideous screeching sound again. The stadium once more plunges into darkness, and when the lights rise again, the figure is gone. Bryan: Oh, that noise is horrible! And just like that the man in black is gone again! Whoever it is, he's just completely ruined the NC-17 opening bout! Yale: And he took out two tag teams single-handedly! First he helps Garbage Bag Johnny, then he destroys half the tag division... what the hell is this person's agenda? What could he possibly want?! Bryan: This makes no sense to me! Someone needs to find out who this person is and put an end to this, because that's two big matches that have been ruined! Yale: I don't know that I'd say this was ruined... I actually kinda found that entertaining. Bryan: You would. The fact remains that this is not an acceptable way to kick off this Pay-Per-View! Yale: I really want to know who this is, JB... someone's definitely trying to make a statement, even if it's not clear what that statement is yet. Bryan: Well, fans, we apologize for this... we'll get the ring cleared and be back shortly for what I hope will be our first uninterrupted match of the evening! Right now I'm getting word that we have Sally Ford in the back and there's some sort've commotion in the backstage area. ---- A System-X Scavenger Hunt Backstage we find Sally Ford strutting through a hallway. The crowd whistles as she appears on the MegaTron, and she suddenly stops as is having heard the reaction. In fact she seems to have heard some noise coming from a nearby room. Raising an eyebrow she investigates, finding the ruckus to be emanating from an office marked "GCW Commissioner Christian Zenith." Yale: What's all this about? Pressing her ear against the door, Ford listens at the sound of rustling papers and sliding furniture. She tentatively knocks, and suddenly the noise inside ceases. A pair of muffled voices can be heard inside. Voice: The hell? Is he back already? Voice: I'll get the bat. Suddenly the door swings open, and a familiar face is revealed in the doorway. Bryan: Hey! It's The Jhub!! Yale: What?! What's HE doing in Zenith's office?! The crowd roars as The Jhub glances back over his shoulder, careful to block the camera's view of the interior of the room. He finally gets a full look at Sally Ford, however, and cocks an eyebrow. He smooths back his buzz-cut hair and adjusts an imaginary tie. The Jhub: Well hello there. Ford: Oh, uh, hi... You're The Jhub! The Jhub: Well, yes, but you can call me "The" for short. Ford: You're in Commissioner Zenith's office. The banging and rustling resumes in the background. The Jhub: Oh, this is Zenith's office? I thought this was the john. We're gonna need a janitor in here or something... Ford: Who's in there with you? What's going on? What are you doing here? The Jhub: You sure ask a lot of questions for a hot chick in wrestling, you know that? Ford: It's kinda my job. The Jhub: Really? You mean you're not just here until they talk you into posing for Playboy? Before Ford can respond, a hand emerges over Jhub's shoulder clutching a sheet of paper. The Jhub examines it for a moment but shakes his head. The Jhub: No, this won't do it. Try to find something in a vial, or like a cryogenically frozen alien fetus. A grunt of dismay follows from The Jhub's friend, and the rustling and banging resumes. Ford: What are you looking for? The Jhub: Evidence. He nods for emphasis. Ford: What kind of evidence? The Jhub: Oh, there's all kinds of evidence. DNA evidence, hair follicles, fingerprints, semen... Ford: You're looking for semen? The Jhub: Why? Are... you looking for some semen? The Jhub smiles at her again, but the door swings open a bit wider and interrupts the conversation. Another cheer goes up as Toad appears beside The Jhub! Bryan: Hey! It's the former Head of GCW Security, ten time Hardcore Champion Toad! Yale: Zenith fired him! He's got no right to be here! Toad: There's no evidence here. It must be somewhere else. Who's the broad? The Jhub: Oh, this is... what did you say your name was, honey? Sally opens her mouth to answer. The Jhub: Whatever. Look, we're on a bit of a scavenger hunt, if you will, System-X style. We're looking for something, anything, that will get my boy Toad here his job back. And we're not going to stop until we find it! Toad: Well, we're not gonna find it here. The place is clean. The Jhub frowns and the two depart. As they exit the screen, the open door reveals the interior of Zenith's office. His desk has been broken in two, the entire floor is littered wall to wall with scattered papers, and a small fire is burning in the corner. Bryan: Uh... wow. Yale: I... wha... buh... You've got to be kidding me! They completely demolished Zenith's office! He's gonna be pissed! Ford watches in stunned silence as The Jhub and Toad exit. She shakes her head and turns to leave, but nearly walks right into the oncoming Commissioner. Zenith: Excuse me. Ford, unable to find the correct words, simply backs out of Zenith's way. Zenith eyeballs her as he steps to his office, only to see the destruction that has taken place inside. At once his shoulders slump and a deep breath of air escapes his lips. Zenith: You have GOT to be kidding me. He turns to face the camera, his face blank. Finally his lips contort into a frown, and he slams his eyes shut for extra force on his next words. Zenith: SECURITY!!!! Bryan: Wow! Zenith is furious! System-X has destroyed his office! Yale: I don't know what they think they're going to find, either, JB! An alien fetus?! Bryan: Either way, The Jhub and Toad are on a mission to make Toad the head of GCW Security once more! And from the look of things, Zenith could use the help! Yale: Wait, you're saying Zenith should hire Toad as head of Security in order to prevent people like Toad from breaking things? Bryan: Right. Yale: Just checking... don't we have a match coming up here next? ---- Dan Black vs. Mr. Danger vs. AXEL Action vs. 'The Prophet' John Adams Bryan: Indeed we do, Dave. I think we can say we're already off to an unexpected start after the match between Vassago & Cleaopatra and Samson & Delylah was interrupted by The Man In Black. Now we move over with a complete change of pace as we go into singles action hoping we can get this one in the books cleanly with a winner. Yale: Well its not too entirely different than the first bout. There are still four competitors in the ring, except this time they're all going to be at each others throats. Bryan: So we'd think. You can never second judge the antics of Dan Black and Mr. Danger. Yale: With the Movement officially breaking up last week on Worldwide, I'm shocked to even still see these two in an alliance. I was waiting for the blame game to start getting passed around. Bryan: Black seems to be looking to Mr. Danger confidently for direction and advice. Their relationship could very well be the key to getting Black into the United States Championship division and the first duel title holder! Yale: Let's take it one step at a time, JB! Andrews: The following contest is a four way dance and is for the Global Championship Wrestling Television Championship!! Introducing first, from Hollywood, Florida weighing in at two hundred and twenty five pounds.. AXXXEEEELLL AAAAAAAAAAACCCCTTTTTIIIOOOOON! "Waiting for the Heavens" by Eighteen Visions begins to play over the in-house audio system. The lights dim and the outline of a man in a hooded robe emerges from behind the curtain to rather mixed reaction of both cheers and boos. As the first strain of the opening guitar riff hits, he thrusts his arms out to the side and throws his head back in a crucifix pose as pillars of flame explode along the perimeter of the stage. Bryan: This match means a lot for Axel Action. After conquering a singles career, he claims he left the Movement simply because he was being held back yet again. Yale: A loss here tonight would really make him look foolish then, wouldn't it? In the light of the fire, the robe is shown to be black in color with intricate red designs that bear silver linings. He flips the hood back to reveal himself as none other than The Unholy One, himself, Axel Action. He saunters to the ring with a look of determination on his face and a purpose in his step. He climbs the ring steps and makes his way onto the apron, turning to face the crowd. AXEL thrusts his arms out to the sides once more before entering the ring and removing his robe. Bryan: The fans not entirely sure how they should react to the Unholy One. I don't think he's particularly won their hearts over the years. Yale: I really don't even think he's wanted to, either, JB. I think Axel is just happy being Axel. Andrews: And his opponent, from Riverside, California standing tall at seven foot three. JOHHHHNN THE PROPHEEEETTT AAAAAAAAAAAAADDDDAAAAAMMMMMSSSS!!! "Dead Man Walking" by Bloodsimple starts up over the PA system as the monster John Adams steps through the curtains and out onto the stage. The fans respond with a big pop for The Prophet as he slowly approaches the ring without any fancy pyrotechnic work. Bryan: This match was originally intended to actually be a singles bout between Dan Black and John Adams not much more than a month ago. It's crazy to see how it evolved. Yale: From a fan's standpoint, it’s the best thing that could've ever happened. But from the standpoint of Dan Black and John Adams -- it just sucks! Bryan: That's absolutely right. Neither Dan nor The Prophet have to be pinned to lose the match. So what was a match based on pure in-ring wrestling skill now requires so much more! Adams climbs over the tope rope and into the ring, staring down Axel Action. The two superstars remain on opposite sides of the ring while Joey Andrews calls for the next participant. Andrews: And their opponent..from Kansas City, Missouri. MIISTTTTTTEEEEEEEEEEEEEEEEERRR DAAANNNGGEEEEERRR!! Mr. Danger steps out onto the stage to two huge blue pyro explosives and a chorus of boos. He makes it only halfway down the ramp until he stops in his tracks, realizing that going in the ring alone would be suicide. Bryan: It looks as if Danger is playing it safe right now. Yale: I certainly would, too. Bryan: It'd probably be much more comforting going into that ring with the massive Dan Black watching your back. And almost on cue... Andrews: And finally, their opponent. He is the current record setting GCW Television Champion. Hailing from New York City... DDAAAAAANNNN THE BULLDOZER BLLAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAACCCCCKKKKKK!!! "The Ultimate Remix" by Jarrid Mendelson kicks up throughout the arena as the chorus of booing grows louder. Dan Black tears through the curtain and onto the stage with the TV title wrapped firmly around his waist. He marches down the ramp, stopping alongside Mr. Danger to survey the ring. Bryan: I guess that confirms any presumptions that these two would be in this together. Yale: Looks like we could very well be seeing Dan Black retain tonight! On cue, Black and Danger continue down the second half of the ramp with increasing speed. Both men dive underneath the bottom rope and pop right up to their feet. Ding, ding, ding Referee David Fellows calls for the opening bell as the match instantly kicks off. Just as Black and Danger get to their feet, they're met by John Adams and Axel Action. Adams squares off with Black while Danger meets Axel. Adams, who towers Black by almost a full foot, hammers down on The Bulldozer with a series of overhand chops to the inside shoulder. Black responds with quick jabs into Adams' midsection, which seem to have on affect on The Prophet. Bryan: You'd think John Adams was wearing a shirt of steel with the way he's not even flinching at Black's strikes. Yale: The scary thing is that Black is a big boy. I don't know how Adams is just sucking those hits up. The Prophet follows through an overhand smash with a back elbow, sending the champion staggering a few steps backward. Black steps back into Adams, looking to mount another offensive, but eats up another back elbow smash from The Prophet. The Bulldozer stumbles back once more, this time grabbing his head. Adams grabs hold of Dan Black by the wrist and the shoulder and sends him across the ring to the ropes. Black hits the ropes and heads back towards Adams. Adams lifts his massive leg high up into the air, looking for a boot to the face. Black ducks underneath and continues all the way through to the other side. He hits the ropes and comes back at Adams full speed. The Prophet turns and gets driven in the chest and straight down to the canvas by the champion! Bryan: And what a spear by the Television Champion! Yale: Way to turn nothing into something! I thought for sure that Dan Black was about to have his head removed with John Adams' boot. Bryan: Oh, yeah. I got nervous for the Bulldozer for a minute. On the other side of the ring, the action (no pun intended) continues with Axel Action and Mr. Danger. Danger takes the immediate advance by countering an attempt at a lockup with a low kick to the inner thigh, barely missing the groinal area. Bryan: That's a perfect display of Danger's veteran wrestling ability. He knew he'd get the same effect by kicking Axel in the inner thigh that he would the groin, except wouldn't get disqualified. Yale: If you combine his knowledge of the business with Black's sheer size and strength, you can really develop a brutal duo. Danger wraps Axel's head in a standing headlock and wrenches down on the Unholy One. Axel tries to squirm free but Danger's grip is too strong. Instead, Axel runs forward, forcing Danger forward with him, until he's able to run Danger directly into the corner. Danger's grip loosens enough for Axel to slip his head out and position himself behind Danger. He grabs the back of Danger's head and smashes it against the top turnbuckle. Axel Action steps to the side, allowing Danger to stumble backwards from the corner. The Unholy One then quickly hops up to the second rope and jumps off at Mr. Danger with an inverse bulldog, taking down the veteran. Bryan: Axel Action taking it to the air a bit there, looking to pick up the pace. Yale: That's something you've got to watch for with Axel as of late. He's becoming more and more unorthodox and not following his traditional move set by any means. One minute, he'll be a canvas technician and the next; he'll be soaring through the air with some high risk move. Bryan: Well I’d certainly venture to think if you’d call that one a high risk but I do get the point you’re trying to make. With Danger on his back, Axel looks to go for a quick cover. Yale: I hope so! ---- The Cardinals Did It... The darkness isn't usually the most embraced place. Let's face it; people only embrace the dark for two reasons; sex and sleep. The recesses of blackness also maintain a foothold on things we dislike, reject or refuse to respect. This is true in the world of American Sports. Every single one has their groomed-to-be stars, while they also have their basement dwellers. Teams or stars that aren’t "supposed" to improve, move up, win, shock, etc. It has developed into sports fans ignorance. That it is impossible for anyone to come out from the darkness. But technically, it’s the true beauty of sports. To see which underdog, which long shot actually takes the shot. Clyde Fox was a bottom feeding basement dweller. He was the prototypical "easy win." The game on schedule when you could debate resting your stars. For as long as he lived, Clyde Fox had never experienced that crowning moment in any capacity; a job, a girlfriend, popularity, and God forbid a normal life. There he was though. Somehow proving so many wrong. Sitting poetically in the dark bowels of his locker room. He had been here only once before. Once. That result was a victory. For GCW’s signature event, Fox had claimed a perfect 1-0 debut. But NC-17 2007 couldn’t hold a candle to tonight’s challenge. His greatest challenge in his entire life, no matter what he was doing was coming up in just a few short moments. For a life that could be compared to pebble, Clyde Fox was about to emerge from the darkness and become a solid rock. His crowning moment was surely this night. NC-17 2008; why not him? Why not Clyde Fox? Fox sat there bent over the back of the chair. His head shot straight to the ceiling. Confidence was lacking but desire was manifesting. Fox: Me... why me? A half-heartily whimper about his situation helped relieve some of the stress from Fox. Suddenly Fox lets his head fall as he anxiously wraps his hands together. He takes a deep breath... shuts out all the noise... however, the muffling roar of the crowd would not be outdone. It was his calling. His chance to finally grow up. To show the world that Clyde Fox won’t be just another push over... for at the very least, one night. Everything preceding this means nothing now. It’s one match, one contest, winner take all. Fox: I’m weady... Fox rises from his chair. The glow from around his locker room door invites him to his destiny. Everything has been set up. Indeed, his crowning moment was just about to come... Bryan: Clyde Fox is ready for his match... it's coming up NEXT! ---- Tessa Windsor vs. Clyde Fox The view on the Megatron quickly changes and pans around the arena, cutting from side to side, fan to fan. They hold up signs for an array of GCW superstars. A cluster wave their signs to the camera, yelling at the top of their lungs. The camera focuses in on a sign in the middle of the group in white with bells in the corner saying "Do I Hear Wedding Bells?". The screen cuts to the announcing table, where James Bryan and David Yale sit back, fixing their headsets as they return to air. Bryan: Oh, wedding bells is right--the fans know it and we know it! Clyde Fox and Tessa Windsor's epic struggle is coming to an end tonight, right here in this very ring! The next edition of GCW Radio will return this weekend with a plate full of topics for discussion!! ---- Do The Right Thing Following her defeat at the hands of Clyde Fox, it was far from unusual to see Tessa Windsor marching down the hall with her pastel pink Adidas gym bag slung over her shoulder. Bryan: OH…MY…GOD! Yale: How did he do that?! How in the hell did he do that?! Knight just manhandled Jordan there! Bryan: How does he still have that kind of strength?! Knight just wrenched White around like a ragdoll and planted his skull into the canvas! I think White’s out! Knight’s gonna steal the title! Knight tries to capitalize but is too weary to make the cover. Instead he crawls away and pulls himself up in the ropes, while White struggles to all fours. Knight sees him rising and charges, but White launches himself into the air, leapfrogging him cleanly. Knight hits the ropes again as White springboards in front of him. White flies into Knight, who again overpowers him in the air, drops back, and delivers another huge KnightFall! Bryan: KNIGHTFALL! HE HIT IT AGAIN! Yale: NEW CHAMPION! NEW CHAMPION! Bryan: It’s all over! Knight hit it! Here’s the cover! Even the horrified crowd can’t help but count along… …1! Crowd: ONE!!!! …2!! Crowd: TWO!!!! …At the last moment, Jordan White’s shoulder narrowly slips off the mat. Briggs heroically stops the count from happening, signaling a two to the timekeeper! The crowd explodes, rumbling the roof as Knight slumps into the canvas in despair. Bryan: HE KICKED OUT! WHITE IS ALIVE! Yale: How is he doing this? Bryan: White has kicked out of TWO KnightFalls! LeStatt Knight is absolutely stunned! He’s got no idea what to do from here, he’s given White everything he can! This is a myth-making performance by Jordan White! Yale: But there’s no way he can come back from this! Knight’s just got to put him away, it’s academic at this point! Bryan: Damnit, you may be right, but what a legendary effort Jordan White’s giving us here tonight! Touching the staples still tenuously holding his head together, Knight gets back to his feet and circles the ring. He eyes the crowd, sensing that the end is near, relishing the mighty boos coming his way. Bryan: These fans are letting Knight know exactly what they think of him, but Knight doesn’t give a damn! He can sense his destiny is near! Yale: This is his moment, JB, and he’s going to relish it! He’s about to become only the second three-time champion in GCW history! This is it, his legacy is complete! Barely conscious, White rises, but Knight greets him with a right hand that wobbles the knees. White slumps back against the ropes and bounces back, eating another sharp blow. Knight lifts him high overhead, powering him into a guerilla press. He parades White around the ring for a moment, but White wriggles free and flies out of the ring! Somehow he lands in the middle of the top rope and balances himself in place, causing both the crowd and Knight to freeze in awe! White moonsaults back, hooking Knight’s head and delivering a soaring reverse DDT! Bryan: OH MY GOD! What a move… what a jaw-dropping move by Jordan White! Yale: I have NEVER seen that before, JB! There’s no way he just did that! He was standing on that top rope for a full second! Bryan: White broke free and just pulled off one of the damnedest moves I’ve ever seen! He’s got Knight down! He’s got Knight out! Can he capitalize?! “JOR-DAN! *clap clap* JOR-DAN!” comes the chant from the crowd, desperately trying to give its last bit of energy to the champion. White grits his teeth and stares out at the fans from behind a crimson mask, searching his depths for something in reserve. Knight begins to stir, but White beats him to his feet and hits the ropes. Bryan: White’s trying for one last effort! This is it! Knight reaches his feet just as White leaps in. Knight surges up and catches White, locking him in place for the KnightFall! Bryan: HE’S GOT HIM AGAIN!!! He tries to lift White, but White won’t be denied. He plants his feet and lifts Knight off the mat, simply shoving him away. Knight hits the ropes in an odd direction, his body forcing the top rope down, catching his arms between the top and middle ropes! Stuck, Knight can do nothing as White backs up a step, takes aim, and fires a straight boot right into his face. Bryan: SUPERKICK!! OH MY GOD! Knight’s trapped and White just caught him with the Superkick! Yale: Knight’s got no way out! He’s out cold! The crowd thunders as White backs up and measures a senseless and trapped LeStatt, delivering a second, more emphatic kick to Knight’s face. Bryan: ANOTHER KICK! Jordan White is obliterating the helpless challenger! Yale: SOMEONE STOP THIS! White backs up again, hesitating as he eyes the broken man in front of him. White’s staples have ruptured, hanging from his broken skin as a wave of blood rushes down his face and chest. Gathering all his remaining strength, White steps up and delivers the superkick for a third time. Bryan: THERE’S THE KNOCKOUT BLOW! Just before the blow connects, Knight flips his feet off the mat. He delivers a devastating mule kick to White’s face, cutting him down to the canvas. The impact of the blow allows Knight to flip himself over the top rope, freeing himself from his prison. Knight immediately launches himself onto the top rope and springs off, flying down with a deep frog splash that catches White square in the chest. Bryan: KNIGHTTYME!!!!! OH MY GOD WHAT A COUNTER!!!!!! Yale: NEW CHAMPION! NEW CHAMPION! Bryan: COVER! The awestruck crowd watches on as Briggs dives in for the count! …1!!!! …2!!!!!! …This time there’s no kickout. White remains motionless on the mat as Briggs’s hand hits the mat for the final time. Yale: NEW CHAMPION!!!!! Bryan: HE DID IT! LeSTATT KNIGHT HAS WON THE TITLE! The bell sounds as the crowd melts down. “O’Fortuna” is barely audible over the reaction as Knight, knocked senseless, drops to the mat and stays there. A breathless Briggs remains seated on the mat before turning to look for the belt. Andrews: Here is your winner…….. and the NEEEEEEEWWWW GCW WORLD HEAVYWEIGHT CHAMPION…….. LeSTAAAAAT KNIIIIIIIIIIGHT!!!!!!! Bryan: HE’S DONE IT! LeSTATT KNIGHT HAS WON THE DAMN TITLE! Yale: I knew he could do it! I knew it! I knew it! What a match! What a war! Bryan: LeStatt Knight has become a three-time GCW World Champion! He had to go further, dig deeper, endure more pain and punishment than I have ever seen him take before! But damn it all, LeStatt Knight has defeated Jordan White and claimed the title! Neither man moves as Briggs is handed the title. He tentatively stands in the corner, waiting for Knight to move, but he doesn’t stir. Yale: I’m speechless, JB! What a match! Bryan: And LeStatt can’t even celebrate! He took two straight superkicks from White while tangled in the ropes, and I think he might have been unconscious when he made that last counter! I honestly think he did that on pure instinct alone! Yale: Whatever it was, it was an amazing reversal! It looked like White was going to bludgeon him into oblivion, JB! And he wasn’t going to stop, either! He just kept going! Bryan: And he may have gone for one too many! I really think Knight was out cold, but something clicked in his head and he hit the KnightTyme for the win! The entire ring is spackled with blood, our table is in shambles, LeStatt has staples hanging from his flesh, and at the end of this battle LeStatt Knight has claimed the belt! Finally, after what seems like an eternity, LeStatt confirms he’s alive by twitching his foot. The convulsions travel up his body until he’s in motion, his arm grasping at air in a search for some support. Beside him, White doesn’t move an inch. Bryan: Neither one of these men is going anywhere! We need some serious medical attention down here! Yale: I’ll give the son of a bitch his due, JB! Jordan White just put on one hell of a show! He did things in that ring I didn’t think were possible! Bryan: Bow down to that man, ladies and gentlemen, because Jordan White proved he was up for this all the way! There was no fear, no intimidation, no hesitation in his performance! He left it all in the ring and proved to be LeStatt Knight’s equal! But in the end LeStatt just pulled this one out of thin air! Yale: He didn’t pull it out of thin air, JB! It was his destiny reaching down from heaven and manifesting itself before our eyes! There was only one way this match was going to end, and Jordan White was not going to deny LeStatt his legacy! Bryan: And where does this leave Jordan’s legacy? He proved he’s capable of wrestling at an insane, Hall of Fame level here tonight, but he’s lost his title? What on earth is this going to do to the life of Jordan White? Yale: Nobody emerges from these wars with LeStatt the same again, JB! It took Jason Dread two years to get back in the ring. Dawn McFarlane never recovered. Michael Stevens hasn’t been heard from. If the pattern continues, we may never see Jordan White again! Bryan: This kid’s got way too bright a future for that, Dave! Did you just watch that match?! He put LeStatt Knight through the worst kind of hell he’s ever experienced! This kid is destined to be back here again, I guarantee you that! Knight finally gets to his knees and sees Briggs standing nearby, the GCW Title belt shimmering in the spotlight. The sight of the gold revives Knight, and though his legs nearly fail him, he rushes to the corner and snatches the belt away. Knight brings it to his chest, his glassy eyes aimed out at the crowd. Finally he slumps against the turnbuckle and thrusts the belt into the air, setting off a wave of frenetic camera flashes. Bryan: And there it is! LeStatt Knight is on top of GCW again! Yale: And everything’s right with the world! The Celtics are NBA Champions, the Steelers are NFL Champions, and LeStatt Knight is GCW Champion! All the dynasties are back in place! Bryan: What a war! Never in my life have I seen something so intense, such a back and forth test of wills! Neither of these men was willing to quit, this match changed direction more times than I can count! LeStatt just happened to have the good fortune of being on top when the final count fell! Yale: Call it fortune, call it destiny, call it whatever you want, JB! LeStatt Knight has been on a mission that started with Dangerous Games, and he’s come in here and done the one thing he’d never done before! There is now absolutely nothing this man hasn’t done, and he’s the GCW Champion for a third time! Knight painfully crosses the ring and lifts himself onto the second rope. Again he raises the belt into the air, roaring in celebration. The crowd, disgusted and breathless, nevertheless captures the historic moment with a deluge of cameras. Bryan: There is it! The new GCW Champion, LeStatt Knight reigns supreme again! Ladies and gentlemen, we’re out of time from NC-17 2008! Thank you and good night! The Pay-Per-View broadcast comes to a close as LeStatt Knight, drenched in sweat and blood, slings the GCW Title belt back in its place over his shoulder, and he casts a glance down at the fallen former champion……
